# Complete Directory of Public APIs Integrated into MYRA

**Source Repository**: [public-apis/public-apis](https://github.com/public-apis/public-apis)
**Total Categories**: 51
**Total Integrated APIs**: 1756

This directory contains the exhaustive catalog of all public APIs integrated into MYRA.
MYRA uses this catalog along with semantic reasoning to automatically select, recommend,
and invoke the most appropriate API for any user request or background task.
